              "patch": "Index: weather-app/README.md\n===================================================================\n--- weather-app/README.md\t\n+++ weather-app/README.md\t\n@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@\n+# Aurora Weather — Full-stack Next.js + Tailwind\n+\n+Modern weather web app built with the **Next.js App Router (full-stack)** + **Tailwind CSS v4** + **lucide-react icons**.\n+Live, free and open data from **Open-Meteo** — no API key required.\n+\n+## Features (as requested + extras)\n+\n+- **Current weather**: temperature, feels-like, condition, humidity, wind speed\n+- Plus: wind direction/gust, pressure, cloud cover, precipitation, UV, sunrise/sunset\n+- **Search any city/location** with autocomplete (`/api/geocode` → Open-Meteo Geocoding)\n+- **°C / °F toggle**, quick-pick cities, favorites (localStorage), geolocation “Locate me” with reverse-geocode label\n+- **Hourly (24h) strip + 7-day forecast** (`/api/weather` → Open-Meteo Forecast)\n+- Dynamic gradient background by weather code + day/night, glassmorphism, responsive, dark-modern UI\n+\n+## Architecture (full-stack)\n+\n+```\n+src/app/page.tsx              # client UI\n+src/app/api/weather/route.ts  # GET ?latitude=&longitude=&name=&country=&admin1= → Open-Meteo forecast\n+src/app/api/geocode/route.ts  # GET ?name=&count= → Open-Meteo geocoding search\n+src/lib/weather.ts            # types, WMO mapping, unit helpers\n+```\n+\n+API routes add validation, `Cache-Control` + Next `revalidate` caching, and keep the Open-Meteo upstream calls server-side.\n+\n+Data sources:\n+- Forecast: `https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?...` (CC-BY 4.0)\n+- Geocoding: `https://geocoding-api.open-meteo.com/v1/search?...`\n+- Reverse label (browser only): `api.bigdatacloud.net` free client API\n+\n+## Getting Started\n+\n+```bash\n+cd weather-app\n+npm install\n+npm run dev\n+```\n+\n+Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000).\n+\n+```bash\n+npm run lint\n+npm run build\n+npm start\n+```\n+\n+## Deploy\n+\n+Any Node host / Vercel works with `npm run build && npm start`. No env vars needed.\n",

undefined;\n+  const country = searchParams.get(\"country\") ?? undefined;\n+  const admin1 = searchParams.get(\"admin1\") ?? undefined;\n+\n+  const latitude = Number(lat);\n+  const longitude = Number(lon);\n+\n+  if (!lat || !lon || Number.isNaN(latitude) || Number.isNaN(longitude)) {\n+    return NextResponse.json(\n+      { error: \"Missing or invalid latitude/longitude\" },\n+      { status: 400 }\n+    );\n+  }\n+  if (latitude < -90 || latitude > 90 || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180) {\n+    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Coordinates out of range\" }, { status: 400 });\n+  }\n+\n+  const url =\n+    `https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${latitude}&longitude=${longitude}` +\n+    `&current=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,apparent_temperature,is_day,precipitation,weather_code,cloud_cover,pressure_msl,wind_speed_10m,wind_direction_10m,wind_gusts_10m` +\n+    `&hourly=temperature_2m,precipitation_probability,weather_code` +\n+    `&daily=weather_code,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,sunrise,sunset,uv_index_max,precipitation_probability_max` +\n+    `&timezone=auto&forecast_days=7`;\n+\n+  try {\n+    const res = await fetch(url, { next: { revalidate: 600 } });\n+    if (!res.ok) {\n+      return NextResponse.json(\n+        { error: \"Upstream weather provider failed\" },\n+        { status: 502 }\n+      );\n+    }\n+    const data = await res.json();\n+    if (name) {\n+      data.place = { name, country, admin1 };\n+    }\n+    return NextResponse.json(data, {\n+      headers: {\n+        \"Cache-Control\": \"public, s-maxage=600, stale-while-revalidate=300\",\n+      },\n+    });\n+  } catch {\n+    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Failed to fetch weather\" }, { status: 500 });\n+  }\n+}\n",

weather-app/src/app/page.tsx\t\n@@ -0,0 +1,646 @@\n+\"use client\";\n+/* eslint-disable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect -- data-fetch + autocomplete effects legitimately sync async results into state Sunrise,\n+  Sunset,\n+  Thermometer,\n+  Umbrella,\n+  Wind,\n+  X,\n+  Loader2,\n+  CloudRainWind,\n+} from \"lucide-react\";\n+import {\n+  GeoPlace,\n+  WeatherResponse,\n+  backgroundFor,\n+  fmtTemp,\n+  toF,\n+  weatherCodeInfo,\n+  windDirection,\n+} from \"@/lib/weather\";\n+\n+type Unit = \"C\" | \"F\";\n+\n+interface FavPlace {\n+  name: string;\n+  country?: string;\n+  admin1?: string;\n+  latitude: number;\n+  longitude: number;\n+}\n+\n+const DEFAULT_PLACES: FavPlace[] = [\n+  { name: \"Berlin\", country: \"Germany\", latitude: 52.52, longitude: 13.41 },\n+  { name: \"New York\", country: \"United States\", latitude: 40.71, longitude: -74.0 },\n+  { name: \"Tokyo\", country: \"Japan\", latitude: 35.68, longitude: 139.69 },\n+  { name: \"London\", country: \"United Kingdom\", latitude: 51.5, longitude: -0.12 },\n+];\n+\n+function useDebounced(value: string, delay = 400) {\n+  const [v, setV] = useState(value);\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    const t = setTimeout(() => setV(value), delay);\n+    return () => clearTimeout(t);\n+  }, [value, delay]);\n+  return v;\n+}\n+\n+export default function Home() {\n+  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\"\");\n+  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<GeoPlace[]>([]);\n+  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);\n+  const [showDrop, setShowDrop] = useState(false);\n+  const [place, setPlace] = useState<FavPlace>(DEFAULT_PLACES[0]);\n+  const [weather, setWeather] = useState<WeatherResponse | null>(null);\n+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n+  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<Unit>(() => {\n+    try {\n+      const u = localStorage.getItem(\"aurora:unit\");\n+      return u === \"F\" ? \"F\" : \"C\";\n+    } catch {\n+      return \"C\";\n+    }\n+  });\n+  const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<FavPlace[]>(() => {\n+    try {\n+      const f = localStorage.getItem(\"aurora:favorites\");\n+      return f ? (JSON.parse(f) as FavPlace[]) : [];\n+    } catch {\n+      return [];\n+    }\n+  });\n+  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false);\n+  const boxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n+\n+  const debouncedQuery = useDebounced(query);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    localStorage.setItem(\"aurora:favorites\", JSON.stringify(favorites));\n+  }, [favorites]);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    localStorage.setItem(\"aurora:unit\", unit);\n+  }, [unit]);\n+\n+  // close dropdown on outside click\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    const fn = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n+      if (boxRef.current && !boxRef.current.contains(e.target as Node))\n+        setShowDrop(false);\n+    };\n+    document.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", fn);\n+    return () => document.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", fn);\n+  }, []);\n+\n+  // autocomplete\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    if (debouncedQuery.trim().length < 2) {\n+      setSuggestions([]);\n+      return;\n+    }\n+    let cancelled = false;\n+    setSearching(true);\n+    fetch(`/api/geocode?name=${encodeURIComponent(debouncedQuery)}&count=6`)\n+      .then((r) => r.json())\n+      .then((d) => {\n+        if (!cancelled) {\n+          setSuggestions(d.results ?? []);\n+          setShowDrop(true);\n+        }\n+      })\n+      .catch(() => {})\n+      .finally(() => {\n+        if (!cancelled) setSearching(false);\n+      });\n+    return () => {\n+      cancelled = true;\n+    };\n+  }, [debouncedQuery]);\n+\n+  const fetchWeather = useCallback(async (p: FavPlace) => {\n+    setLoading(true);\n+    setError(null);\n+    try {\n+      const params = new URLSearchParams({\n+        latitude: String(p.latitude),\n+        longitude: String(p.longitude),\n+        name: p.name,\n+        country: p.country ?? \"\",\n+        admin1: p.admin1 ?? \"\",\n+      });\n+      const res = await fetch(`/api/weather?${params.toString()}`);\n+      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(\"Weather request failed\");\n+      const data: WeatherResponse = await res.json();\n+      setWeather(data);\n+    } catch (e) {\n+      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : \"Could not load weather\");\n+    } finally {\n+      setLoading(false);\n+    }\n+  }, []);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    fetchWeather(place);\n+  }, [place, fetchWeather]);\n+\n+  const selectPlace = (p: FavPlace) => {\n+    setPlace(p);\n+    setQuery(\"\");\n+    setSuggestions([]);\n+    setShowDrop(false);\n+    // recent\n+    try {\n+      const raw = localStorage.getItem(\"aurora:recent\");\n+      const arr: FavPlace[] = raw ?
